  Frequency column is read-only, sourced live from the Product's own
  `billing_frequency` (never a per-benefit field, matching stage 1's schema).
  New selections come from active, tenant-scoped Products only; an item
  already selected on a promotion stays visible/editable even after going
  inactive (fixed a stage-2 gap: `PUT` on the three endpoints now only
  requires *newly* added `gym_charge_id`s to be active, mirroring the Suitable
  Membership Plans `/plans` PUT pattern) — this was the ticket's explicit
  "existing selected items must remain visible when editing" requirement.
  Backend: the legacy `charge_types`-pseudo-catalog-driven `/period-benefits`
  (bulk + single-row) and `/included-benefits` endpoints are removed outright;
  `promotion_period_benefits` stays (still backs the untouched
  `/membership-fee-benefit` singleton, #551) and `promotion_included_benefits`
  stays but is now unused (no remaining write path; no data loss beyond what
  migration 158 already reset, per the issue owner's "start from scratch"
  clarification). New/updated tests in `promotions.test.ts` (removed tests for
  the retired endpoints, added the "already-selected item stays selectable
  after going inactive" case for all three categories) and `gym-charges.test.ts`
  (`benefit_category` classification + presence on list/detail/create/update).

- **Done**: #592 (Link Staff records to auth identities; Impersonate lists Staff; remove Team — root cause of the empty Admin Impersonate dialog: `GET /platform/impersonation/targets?type=staff` reads `gym_memberships`, but the Staff page reads the `staff` table and nothing ever wrote `staff.gym_membership_id` (present since #123), while the only UI that created `gym_memberships` rows — the Team page — had been dropped from navigation in #77 and was orphaned. No migration. `api/src/infra/staff-access.ts` now holds the Clerk invite/grant/revoke/link mechanics moved from the deleted `gym-users.ts`; `POST /staff` grants access on create with the role fixed by `PROFILE_ROLE_MAP` (profile → RBAC role, `infra/permissions.ts`, mirrored in `apps/admin/src/config/permissions.ts`) and stores the link; `PUT` syncs role/name (self + last-admin guards); deactivate revokes the login (Clerk user kept); delete revokes and removes the Clerk user when it was their last gym; new `POST/DELETE /staff/:id/access` for retry/resend/revoke; `POST /staff/link` replaces `/gym-users/link` (`GymContext` self-heal repointed; webhook import repointed). Impersonation staff targets are `gym_memberships LEFT JOIN staff`, returning the Staff name + `profile`; gym-owner admin rows (no Staff record) stay impersonable. Admin: `/team` + `/link-team` pages and the `team` i18n namespace removed; Staff row's Clerk section became **App access** (status, role, Send/Resend invitation, Revoke) with a role hint under the Profile select; `ImpersonationDialog` shows the profile. Tests: new `staff-access.test.ts` (auth/tenant guards, grant-existing/invite paths, unknown profile 400, inactive-on-create, Clerk failure keeps the row, resend overwrites `invitation_id`, PUT role/name sync, last-admin + self guards, deactivate/delete/revoke semantics), `impersonation.test.ts` extended for the join, `require-role.test.ts` retargeted to `/staff`, `gym-users.test.ts` deleted.)

- **Done**: #594 (Refuse member/staff email collision within a gym — surfaced while verifying #592: granting staff access to an email that is already a member of the same gym silently `UPDATE`d the single `gym_memberships` row's role to the staff role, breaking the member app for that person (`requireRole('member')` 403s) with nothing visible in the admin UI, and a later revoke deleted the row with no way back (`/me/link` requires `clerk_user_id IS NULL`). Intended model written down: staff log in with their work account, membership lives on a personal account. Both sides now refuse with 409: `assertNotMemberEmail` (staff side, runs before `POST /staff` inserts anything, on `PUT` email change, and inside `grantAccess`) and `isStaffLoginEmail` (member side, `POST /members` + `POST /members/:id/invite`), plus `POST /me/link` refuses when the account already holds a non-member row in the gym. No migration, no UI change beyond the error message. New `api/src/test/member-staff-collision.test.ts` (10 cases, both directions incl. case-insensitive match, existing-account member row, pending staff invite, and the `/me/link` guard).)

- **Done**: #596 (Admin impersonation — role/gym list stayed stale until reload after start/stop: `GymContext` derived the effective role by reading `sessionStorage` during render instead of subscribing to `ImpersonationContext`, and `GymProvider` gets `children` as a stable prop so the parent's `setSession` never re-rendered it; right after starting a `front_desk` session the Staff page still showed **+ New Staff Member** / **Revoke access** until a reload (UI-only — the API always enforced the real role via `x-impersonate-as`). `GymProvider` now consumes `useImpersonation()`; `ImpersonationContext` gained a `ready` flag (mirrors the Member app, #415) with a synchronous storage fallback before rehydration so a mid-session reload never flashes the superadmin role. Frontend-only, no API/migration. Investigated and ruled out in the same ticket: the session does **not** survive *Stop* — storage is cleared synchronously and stays cleared across navigations; the `ERR_ABORTED` next to `/stop → 204` is just how a bodiless 204 is logged.)

- **Done**: #600 (Removed the leftover one-time `POST /dev/seed-gym` route from `api/src/app.ts` — commented "remove after use" and never removed; it sat outside `requireAuth()`. Nothing referenced it: local seeding is `api/src/infra/seed.ts`, gym creation is the superadmin `POST /platform/gyms`. No migration, no UI change. New `api/src/test/dev-seed-gym.test.ts` (3 cases) pins the route as gone and asserts no gym/membership row is written. Convention going forward: no route is mounted in `app.ts` without `requireAuth()` unless it is deliberately public (`/health`, `/docs`, `/public`, `/payment-page`, `/billing`, `/themes`, webhooks).)

- **Done**: #599 (Website self-registration — a gym's WordPress site can now send the member invite email. New `POST /public/gyms/:slug/registrations` (`{ name, email, center_id?, locale? }`), called server-to-server and authenticated by a per-gym API key in `x-api-key`; only a salted scrypt digest is stored, with a prefix pre-filter so junk never reaches scrypt (migration 159: `gyms.website_api_key_hash/_prefix/_created_at` + flag `system.website_integration`). It only creates a Clerk invitation carrying `publicMetadata.gym_signup`; the `members` row is created on first sign-in by the extended `POST /me/link` (201), so fake requests never reach the roster. Always `202` once authenticated (no enumeration; Clerk `422` swallowed = per-email throttle), one shared `401` for bad key/unknown slug, per-IP limiter before DB work + per-gym limiter after the key check (env-tunable). Admin: new **System → Website Integration** page (`GET /system/website-integration`, `POST`/`DELETE …/key` — key shown once, rotate/revoke audited). Also fixes a pre-existing bug: `trust proxy` was never set behind nginx, so every per-IP limiter (incl. the global 500/15 min) was one bucket for the whole platform (`TRUST_PROXY_HOPS`, default 1). `gyms.ts` reads `g.*`, so `stripGymSecrets` keeps the hash out of responses and audit payloads. WordPress setup: `docs/wordpress-integration.md`. Tests: `public-registrations.test.ts`, `website-integration.test.ts`, `me-link-self-registration.test.ts`, unit `website-api-key.test.ts`.)

- **Done**: #605 (Docs only — new `docs/go-to-production.md`: single checklist of go-live requirements (GitHub `production` environment, Clerk Production instance, first-superadmin bootstrap via Clerk Dashboard `publicMetadata.platform_role` — not `npm run db:seed`, which also creates a placeholder gym —, public-route audit, Monei/PCI items from `decisions.md`). Linked from `README.md`, `docs/architecture.md` and the `CLAUDE.md` doc-update checklist.)

- **Done**: #607 (Staff logins — Impersonate was empty in every gym but Claude MONEI Test because Staff records created before #592 had no login and saving them never created one, and invited staff looked impersonable but weren't. `PUT /staff/:id` now grants a login to an active record without one (same as create, #594 guard included) and removes it when the record is saved inactive (matches `PATCH /deactivate`); response carries `access`, the form warns an invitation email may be sent. Impersonate dialog lists invited staff greyed out as "Invitation pending"; `POST /platform/impersonation/:targetId` refuses them — reverses the POST half of #358, whose test only passed because the Clerk mock resolves any id (`tenantContext` looks the target up in Clerk on every request). No migration. Tests: 5 new PUT cases in `staff-access.test.ts`, pending-target refusal in `impersonation.test.ts`.)

- **Done**: #614 (Calendar permission module in the API — #247 made Calendar an independent permission area with front desk read/write, but only the admin app got it; the API still gated `/calendar-events` + `/class-sessions` on TRAINING, where front desk is `R`, so front desk saw create/edit controls that 403'd. Added `CALENDAR` to the API `PERMISSION_MATRIX` (admin/trainers/front desk RW, nutritionist R, accountant/member NONE) and switched both mounts and all 9 write routes to it. Admin app's nutritionist cell corrected RW → R so UI = API. No migration. New `calendar-permissions.test.ts`; `calendar-events.test.ts` front-desk assertion flipped 403 → 201.)

- **Done**: #609 + #610 (Feature flag coverage — found by running the real `filterNavGroups` once per production flag: Professional Services and Assigned Plans were gated on keys never seeded into `feature_flags` (missing key = enabled → never hideable, absent from Cordel → Feature Flags; `/professional-services` also checked the missing key), and Taxes shared `financials.gym_charges` with Products. Migration 160 seeds `organization.professional_services`, `financials.assigned_plans` and `financials.taxes` (copying gym_charges' current value). Taxes menu entry uses `financials.taxes`; `/taxes` reads now depend only on the `financials` group flag (shared tax dropdown in Plans/Products — hiding Products used to break it) and `financials.taxes` gates its five write routes. New `feature-flag-coverage.test.ts`. Permission-matrix mismatch between the admin app and the API split out as #611.)

- **Done**: #613 + #611 (Read-only roles. Part 1/3 (Organization + Calendar pages): new `useModuleAccess(module)` hook (impersonation-aware), `ContextMenuItem.disabled`, `readOnlyStyle`, `common.read_only_hint`. Centers, Spaces, Activity Types (incl. schedule-rule buttons), Professional Services, Operating Hours (weekly grid + holidays), Staff (controls shown disabled instead of hidden; editor Save gated) and Calendar now disable write controls for roles without write access. New `read-only-writes.test.ts` (51 cases across MEMBERS/ORGANIZATION/TRAINING/NUTRITION/FINANCIALS/PAYMENTS). Client redirects stay until #611. Part 2/3 (Financials + Payments, done): Plans (incl. every in-row billing/centers/allowance/price edit button; *Assign to member* follows PAYMENTS, so front desk keeps it), Promotions, Products, Taxes, and the Assigned Plan row (status actions shown disabled instead of hidden; *Close* admin-only via the hook's new `isAdmin`). Transactions, Billing Events and Payment Providers have no write controls. Part 3/3 (Training + Nutrition, this PR): the seven Training/Nutrition pages switch to the hook, show row actions disabled instead of hidden, and gate Add/New/Import-defaults, Clone and Assign-to-member; hook gains `useReadOnlyTitle`.) **#611**: admin `PERMISSION_MATRIX` aligned cell for cell with the API (= #156 spec): trainers + nutritionist gain Organization, nutritionist gains Training, front desk gains Financials (read), accountant loses Members and is read-only on Financials/Payments. The 15 module pages redirect/return-null/load on `canRead` instead of admin/write checks, so read-only roles open them with write controls disabled. New admin tests `permission-matrix-parity.test.ts` (imports the API table) and `nav-by-role.test.ts`; `apps/admin/vitest.config.ts` adds the `@` alias.)

- **#547 (done)**: Membership Plan Pricing section — one current, VAT-inclusive price per plan plus an immutable price history, per the ticket thread's final clarification ("Price field should be fixed and aligned with the behavior that we have in products… Add Price need to be removed… Save… Apply new price to assigned plans"). Earlier rounds shipped the tax model (#413) and the live net/gross recalculation (#581); this closes the ticket. Migration 161 adds `membership_plan_prices.status` (`active`/`applied`/`inactive`, named CHECK), `applied_at`, and a `tax_rate_id`/`tax_rate_percent` VAT snapshot per price row, backfilled from the window covering today and the plan's current tax rate. New `PUT /membership-plans/:id/pricing` (admin) replaces the plan's current price in one transaction: the superseded row is closed (`valid_to` = yesterday, or its own `valid_from` when it opened today) and marked `inactive`, a new open-ended `active` row opens today with the selected VAT snapshotted, and the plan's `tax_rate_id` is updated with `tax_behavior` pinned to `inclusive` — a VAT change opens a new price too, and re-saving identical values is a no-op. New `POST /membership-plans/:id/pricing/apply-to-assigned-plans` (admin) pushes the current price onto the `user_memberships` still running on the plan (`draft`/`awaiting_payment`/`active`/`paused` only) and marks the price `applied`; terminal (cancelled/expired) Assigned Plans and already-generated `billing_events` are never touched (req. 15), and an Assigned Plan with a `discount_reason` keeps its agreed `final_price` while its plan-price snapshot is refreshed — the response reports `{ updated, kept_discounted }`. `recomputePriceStatuses()` re-derives the status column from the validity windows after every price write (including the legacy `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE /:id/prices` endpoints, kept for API compatibility), so status can never drift from the dates it describes; `effectivePrice()` and `enrichPlan()` break a same-day tie in favour of the non-history row. Admin UI: the expanded Plan row's "Add Price" button and its from/to price-window sub-form are gone, replaced by a **Pricing** section (Price (VAT included) + Tax Rate + **Save**, with the live net/gross preview) and an **Apply new price to assigned plans** button behind a `ConfirmDialog`; the **Price History** section below it is read-only and badges each row Active / Applied to assigned plans / Inactive. Tax Rate and Tax Behavior were removed from the plan's inline edit form (price and VAT are edited together in Pricing, and a plan's price is always VAT-inclusive). Duplication copies each price with its VAT snapshot but never the `applied` marker. Tests: 18 new cases in `api/src/test/membership-plans.test.ts` (auth/role, tenant isolation, cross-tenant tax rate, validation, 0%/21%/10% rates, history immutability, apply-to-assigned including the discount and cancelled-membership cases, duplication) and the updated `plans-pricing-locales.test.ts` key set.

- **#559 (done — stages 1–4)**: Calendar styling section for Base Themes and Custom Themes. **Stage 4** closes the ticket with the accessibility pass: a WCAG-2.1 contrast report under the Calendar section in both theme editors (`apps/admin/src/lib/calendarContrast.ts` + `CalendarContrastReport` in `ThemeTokensEditor.tsx`) that scores every foreground/surface pair the calendar paints — advisory only, never a save blocker, since a gym's branding is its own and `validateTokens()` stays a pure format check; `:focus-visible` indicators for calendar events and nav buttons drawn from tokens guaranteed to read against their surroundings (added identically to Admin's and Member Web's `CalendarThemeStyles.tsx`); and `calendarVarValue()` in both `themeTokens.ts` files hardening the fallback so an *unusable* persisted value (empty or non-hex) falls back to the default rather than dropping the CSS declaration. Covered by `apps/admin/src/test/calendar-a11y.test.ts` and Member Web mirror checks. **Stage 3** finished the event surfaces: an event's fill no longer encodes its status — every event takes the theme's new `calendarEventBackground`/`calendarEventBorder` (joining `calendarEventText`) plus a new `calendarEventHoverBackground` advanced attribute, and the booking status moves to a pill-shaped badge rendered inside the event (`components/CalendarStatusBadge.tsx`, the shape and palette of the app's existing `StatusBadge`). That is the resolution of the conflict with #541 agreed in the thread: status still decides a color and nothing else does, it just paints the badge rather than the box, which is what frees the box to be themeable. `lib/calendarEventColors.ts` remains the single status → color mapping, now returning a `{ bg, fg }` pair and covering `full` (a `scheduled` session at capacity), and the badge labels are translated from `calendar.status_*` instead of showing the raw uppercased DB value. The admin calendar page stops setting a per-event `backgroundColor`/`borderColor` (FullCalendar writes those inline, which would beat the theme); the sheet maps the tokens onto `--fc-event-bg-color`/`--fc-event-border-color`/`--fc-event-text-color` and adds the radius (re-asserting FullCalendar's corner-flattening for continued multi-day events), a hover background and the selected/focused overlay at `opacity: .25`. Member Web is deliberately unchanged — its events are colored by availability to that member via an inline style that wins over these rules. Defaults are the `#6c63ff` purple `scheduled` events already had, so an unconfigured calendar looks as before. **Stage 2** made the stage-1 tokens visible: `applyTokens()` (both `apps/admin/src/lib/themeTokens.ts` and its Member Web mirror) writes one `--gd-calendar-*` CSS variable per calendar token — driven by the new `CALENDAR_COLOR_VARS`/`CALENDAR_ADVANCED_VARS` maps, with every key falling back to its default so a pre-#559 theme still resolves the full set — and a new `CalendarThemeStyles` component holds the FullCalendar override sheet that reads them. The sheet maps each token onto FullCalendar v6's own `--fc-*` variable where one exists (border, page/today/highlight/non-business, the whole nav-button palette), so one declaration reaches `timeGridDay`/`timeGridWeek`/`dayGridMonth` at once, and adds an explicit rule for the surfaces FullCalendar hardcodes (column headers, month-view date numbers including other-month dates, the time axis, slot height, weekend columns). Every rule is scoped to a `gd-calendar` wrapper class on the calendar container in both the admin and member calendar pages, and every `var()` repeats the default token value as its CSS fallback, so an unthemed page renders exactly as before. Event colors are untouched — the three event variables are emitted but read by no rule yet. Two intentional pixel-level changes: other-month dates now use `calendarMutedDayText` at full opacity instead of FullCalendar's `opacity: .3`, and non-business slots paint the configured `calendarDisabledSlotBackground` opaquely instead of FullCalendar's translucent grey (the stage-1 default, `#f7f7f7`, is what that grey renders as over white). Stage 1 added the token layer and the editor section only — 16 `calendar*` colors on `tokens.colors` plus 5 `calendar*` entries in the `advanced` map, their defaults (mirroring FullCalendar's own built-ins so an unconfigured theme is visually unchanged), hex validation in `api/src/domain/themeTokens.ts` (`CALENDAR_COLOR_FIELDS`), and a new `group_calendar` entry in `COLOR_GROUPS`, which gives both theme editors the section at once since they share `ThemeColorsEditor`. No migration: the tokens blob is schema-less JSON and missing keys fall back to defaults, so pre-#559 themes keep saving and rendering unchanged. Per the ticket's clarification thread the Base↔Custom relationship is deliberately untouched — cloning copies the calendar tokens along with everything else, exactly as before; no live sparse inheritance was introduced.

- **Done**: #625 (Limit Membership Fee Benefit duration to the Promotion duration: a Promotion Period Benefit belongs to the Promotion lifecycle and can never outlast it, so `duration_months` is capped at `free_months + paid_months + bonus_months` — Pay Beforehand excluded. Backend rejects an over-long explicit duration on `PUT /promotions/:id/membership-fee-benefit`; the admin inline UI caps the input at the Promotion duration and re-constrains it live when the Promotion is shortened; the Example Timeline forecast uses the effective (clamped) duration so the benefit never reaches the Pay (regular) period even when configured too long or unbounded; migration 162 clamps existing over-long rows. Shared pure helpers `promotionDurationMonths` / `effectiveBenefitDurationMonths`)

- **Done**: #626 (Remove Charge Benefits from Promotions: the Charge Benefits section is gone from the Promotion editor — both the edit form's per-Product action/value grid and the read-only view list — along with the frontend state, handlers and `GET`/`PUT /promotions/:id/charge-benefits` calls that existed only for it, and the `promotions` namespace's Charge Benefit i18n keys. One-off / Session / Period / Membership Fee Benefits and the unrelated Membership Plans Charge Benefits section are untouched. No schema change and no backend removal: `promotion_charge_benefits` is still read at apply time by `membership-promotions.ts` (snapshot + `computeFinalPrice`) and surfaced on Assigned Plans, so the backend is not exclusively required by the removed UI)

- **Done**: #627 (Split Promotion Editing by Section: the Promotion is no longer editable as a whole — the context-menu **Edit** action now only opens the main configuration (General, Suitable Membership Plans, Billing & Duration), and One-off / Session / Period / Membership Fee Benefits each get their own **Edit** button with their own independent Save/Cancel writing only that section's endpoint. A new `editingSection` discriminator alongside `editingId` allows exactly one section of one card to be editable at a time; every other section stays read-only and its Edit button is disabled while one is open. Creating a Promotion stays a single form (no id yet to hang per-section saves off). Frontend-only, inline/expandable UI preserved, no modal; #625's Membership-Fee-duration cap is preserved by re-clamping an over-long saved duration when the main save shortens the Promotion)

- **Done**: #628 (Inline Assign Plan to Member with Promotion selection: the "Assign New Plan" action no longer opens a modal — `AssignNewPlanModal.tsx` is replaced by `AssignPlanInlineEditor.tsx`, rendered inline inside the plan card it supersedes, with plan picker, start date and a new **Promotions** section. Eligible Promotions come from the new `GET /promotions?membership_plan_id=` filter (`EXISTS` over `promotion_membership_plans`) so the list matches what the apply path accepts; the selection enforces the `stackable` rule live (a non-stackable pick disables every other Promotion, any pick disables the non-stackable ones, clearing re-enables all). `POST /user-memberships/:id/assign-new-plan` takes `promotion_ids` and re-validates the whole set server-side before the assignment transaction — shared pure helper `validatePromotionStacking` (`domain/promotionStacking.ts`) plus `validatePromotionSelection` for the per-Promotion eligibility checks — then applies each one through the existing `applyPromotionToMembership`. Membership Plan status workflow untouched; no schema change)

- **Done**: #630 (Rename "Close" to "Cancel": the Assigned Plan context-menu action reads **Cancel** in every locale — label-only, the action still posts to `POST /user-memberships/:id/close`, still runs the two-step confirm (plain, then the unused-value warning) and still maps onto the `cancelled` status. Because the action's own name became "Cancel", the confirm dialogs no longer label their dismiss button with the generic `cancel` key — new `action_close_confirm` / `action_close_dismiss` keys give the two buttons distinct labels. Frontend-only, no API or schema change)

- **Done**: #632 (Collapsible Theme color groups: every group in the Theme editors' **Colors** section (Application, Cards, Text, Separators, Inputs, Header, Sidebar, Navigation, Buttons, Status, Links, Tables, Calendar) now renders as a collapsible card — a full-width header `<button>` carrying the group's existing label, `aria-expanded` and a rotating `▾`, with the group's controls hidden while collapsed. All groups start collapsed. Open state is a local `Set` of group keys in `ThemeColorsEditor`, kept separate from the token draft, so groups toggle independently (not an accordion) and collapsing can never change a color value. Presentation-only: same color pickers, same inline `ThemeAdvancedSection`, same Calendar contrast report, same read-only handling for Base Themes, no new translation keys. Frontend-only, no API or schema change)

- **Done**: #633 (Promotions — new `only_applicable_for_new_members` boolean on the main Promotion configuration: migration 163 adds `promotions.only_applicable_for_new_members tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1` after `stackable`, so existing Promotions are backfilled to `true` by the column default; `promotions.ts` carries it through the list SELECT, POST (defaults to `1` when omitted or null), PUT (`IF(?, ?, …)` partial-update semantics, matching `stackable`) and `POST /:id/duplicate`; the admin Promotions page renders it as a checkbox directly below "Stackable with other promotions", checked by default on create, and the Promotion detail modal shows it read-only. Store-and-display only — Promotion eligibility, stacking, the apply path and the Assign-Plan-to-Member flow are deliberately unchanged, and the `user_membership_promotions` snapshot does not capture it yet; a follow-up ticket implements the behaviour)

- **Done**: #637 (Products column alignment: the list's column headers and every collapsed row now render from one shared `LIST_COLUMNS` definition in `financials/products/page.tsx` — a CSS-grid template (fixed px tracks, the Name column the only flexible one) used by both, replacing the flex rows whose per-cell `minWidth` let a long name or a long "created by" widen its cell and push every column after it out of line with the header. Values ellipsis inside their own track, the chevron and the ⋮ menu share one Actions cell (new `col_actions` label in en/es/ca, the only new key), and the header + rows sit in a single `overflow-x: auto` wrapper with a computed `LIST_MIN_WIDTH`, so a narrow viewport scrolls horizontally instead of dropping columns. Presentation-only: same columns, same order, same data, same actions, no API or schema change)

- **Done**: #638 (Finance Dashboard — Membership Plan cards: the `Finance → Dashboard` page (`[locale]/financials/page.tsx`, until now a "coming soon" placeholder) renders one card per Membership Plan with its name, lifecycle status badge and the number of Assigned Members. New read-only router `api/src/api/financials-dashboard.ts` mounted at `/financials/dashboard` under the `financials` **group** flag (not `financials.plans`, so the Dashboard survives the Plans page being switched off); its single `GET /membership-plans` groups `user_memberships` per plan. A plan is shown when it is `lifecycle_status='active'` or still has at least one assignment, so retired plans members are still on stay visible while empty non-active plans disappear. An assignment counts while it is anything other than `cancelled`/`expired` under the #410 date-aware projection (so the count agrees with the Assigned Plans list), and assignments of soft-deleted members are excluded; each assignment counts once. No migration, no write path, no Membership Plan / Assigned Plan behaviour change. New `api/src/test/financials-dashboard.test.ts` (14 cases: auth, role, tenant isolation, visibility rules, counting invariants); new `financials_dashboard` i18n namespace in en/es/ca)

- **Done**: #639 (Created and Next Payment on Billing Events: `GET /payments/billing-events` rows gain `created_at` (the ledger row's creation instant as a full ISO datetime; `null` on projected rows, which aren't persisted) and `next_payment_date` (`YYYY-MM-DD` of `user_memberships.next_billing_date` for the event's membership, `null` when the event has no membership, the membership is no longer `active`, or nothing is scheduled). The admin Billing Events table renders both as new `Created` / `Next Payment` columns in `DD/MM/YYYY HH:mm`, with `—` for missing data; `next_billing_date` is a DATE column so the scheduled day is rendered at `00:00` rather than parsed as an instant and shifted by the viewer's timezone. Also fixes the future-row projection, which seeded its rolling window with `String(next_billing_date).slice(0, 10)` — mysql2 returns DATE columns as JS `Date` objects, so scheduled rows carried a `billing_date` of `"Wed Oct 21"` instead of an ISO date (rendered as `Invalid Date`, and mis-sorted/mis-filtered against `from`/`to`); a new `toDateOnly()` helper normalises both representations. No migration, no billing-logic, payment-execution or event-status change; new `col_created`/`col_next_payment` i18n keys in en/es/ca; 6 new cases in `payments-billing-events.test.ts`)

- **Done**: #642 (View Audit Log from Member Details: the Members → Details modal gains a **View Audit Log** button that closes the modal and navigates to Configuration → Audit Log already filtered to that member — `/{locale}/audit?entity_type=member&entity_id=<id>`. `AuditLogView` now seeds its `entity_type` / `entity_id` / `entity_name` filters from the query string, and a new **Entity ID** filter box makes the applied filter visible and clearable; `GET /audit-logs` gains a matching exact-match `entity_id` param (served by the existing `(gym_id, entity_type, entity_id)` index — no migration). The button is hidden for non-admins and when the `system.audit` feature flag is off, mirroring the sidebar's rule. New `audit.filter_entity_id*` and `members.action_view_audit_log` i18n keys in en/es/ca; new `audit-logs.test.ts` (8 cases: auth, entity_id filtering, exact match, tenant isolation))

- **Done**: #645 (Registration endpoint keyed by gym id + health check: `POST /public/gyms/:slug/registrations` becomes `POST /public/gyms/:gymRef/registrations`, where `:gymRef` is `{gymId}-{gym-name}` — `parseGymRef` resolves the leading `CHAR(36)` UUID and ignores the readable half, so two gyms with the same name can no longer share an endpoint and a rename never breaks a configured website. A reference that doesn't start with a gym id still resolves as `gyms.slug`, so WordPress installs configured before this change keep registering; `GET /system/website-integration` now advertises the new form (`gym_ref` + `endpoint_path` / `endpoint_url`, name half URL-encoded). Second half: exactly `{ name: 'test', email: '' }` is a health check — after the key check and gym resolution it returns `200 { ok: true, health_check: true }` before validation, creating no member, no invitation and no email, and skipping the per-gym daily quota (per-IP limiter still applies); `isHealthCheckBody` can't collide with a real registration because the schema requires a non-empty email. No migration, no schema change. New unit test `public-registration-gym-ref.test.ts` (13 cases for both helpers), `public-registrations.test.ts` extended with gym-reference and health-check blocks (same-name gyms get non-interchangeable endpoints, legacy slug still 202, probe 401s without a valid key), `website-integration.test.ts` asserts the advertised `gym_ref`; `docs/wordpress-integration.md` documents the new URL format, the health check and a `gymdesk_registration_health_check()` plugin helper; new `website_integration.how_5` i18n key in en/es/ca)

- **Done**: #629 stage 1 (Billing Simulation engine: a read-only, never-persisted forecast of what a Member will actually be charged — `GET /user-memberships/member/:memberId/billing-simulation` returns the concrete dates, the regular price, the Promotion/Benefit that changed it and the resulting amount, consolidated across every simulated assignment of the Member. Member-level rather than per Assigned Plan, per the ticket thread's Q4 answer, so the engine lands once and renders where #634 wants it. Pure engine in `domain/billingSimulation.ts` (sections one-off / year / monthly / 4-week, plus weekly / session / other for cadences the catalogue can produce; Free and Bonus promotional periods waive the fee, Pay/Prepaid apply the Membership Fee Benefit via `computePromotionTimeline`, granted Products are projected at their own `billing_frequency` with 4 weeks as 28 days); loader + router in `api/src/api/billing-simulation.ts`; `loadPromotionApplications` is now exported from `user-memberships.ts` and also carries each Promotion's name and Free/Paid/Bonus/Pay-Beforehand duration. The horizon runs until every item has been charged once at its regular price, capped at 36 months (`truncated`), and charges a year or more out are flagged `price_may_change`. Plan Charge Benefits are deliberately excluded (being decommissioned); Additional Periodic Services (#631) plug into the same engine as stage 2. No migration, no writes — nothing is persisted. New `BILLING SIMULATION` section on the Member expanded row (`MemberBillingSimulation.tsx`), outside the Membership Plan cards; new `members.billing_simulation_*` i18n keys in en/es/ca; 23 unit tests in `billing-simulation.test.ts`, 19 integration cases in `user-memberships.test.ts`, 8 structural cases in the admin app's `member-billing-simulation.test.ts`)

- **Done**: #631 (Additional Periodic Services on an Assigned Plan: migration 164 adds `user_membership_services` (gym_id, `user_membership_id FK→user_memberships` CASCADE, `gym_charge_id FK→gym_charges` with no cascade, quantity, `starts_at`, `ends_at`, CHECKs `quantity > 0` and `ends_at >= starts_at`, plus a VIRTUAL generated `open_service_key` under a unique index so two concurrent POSTs can't attach the same item twice), attaching recurring Products directly to the Assigned Plan — independent of the Membership Plan's included benefits and of Promotion benefits, which keep their own tables. The Product stays the source of truth for name/price/`billing_frequency` (read live, never snapshotted); only items `classifyProduct()` classifies as `periodical` are attachable, and only while the plan still bills. New `api/src/api/user-membership-services.ts` router mounted at `/user-memberships/:id/services` (GET/POST/DELETE, PAYMENTS gate + `payments.transactions` flag); the services are also embedded in `GET /user-memberships/:id` as `additional_services`. Removal is future-only: DELETE stamps `ends_at = today` so charges already produced stand, and only deletes outright when billing hadn't started. The Billing Simulation (#629) now projects each service as its own stream at the item's own frequency and price × quantity, over its effective window, with no Promotion benefit ever applied to it — a service added after the plan started bills from its effective date, and a removed one disappears after its end date. New `ADDITIONAL PERIODIC SERVICES` inline section (no modal) in `AdditionalPeriodicServices.tsx`, rendered both in the Assigned Plan expanded row and in the Member page's current plan card, where an add/remove also remounts the Billing Simulation so it recalculates immediately; new `assigned_plans_page.services_*` / `section_additional_services` and `members.section_additional_services` i18n keys in en/es/ca; 9 new unit cases in `billing-simulation.test.ts`, a new integration file `user-membership-services.test.ts`, 2 new simulation cases in `user-memberships.test.ts`, and 9 structural cases in the admin app's `additional-periodic-services.test.ts`)

- **Done**: #640 (Billing Events Context Menu, Details, Retry Payment and Manual payment: migration 165 adds `modified_at`/`modified_by_user_id` to both `billing_events` and `payment_requests` (Clerk-id actor column, matching `billing_events.actor_user_id`, resolved to a name through `gym_memberships.name`), `failure_code`/`failure_message`/`notes`/`attempt` to `payment_requests`, and widens `chk_payment_requests_source` to `admin|customer|billing_run|retry|manual`. A Billing Event's **status is now derived from its latest linked Payment Transaction** (`domain/billingEventStatus.ts` — pure, unit-tested: `completed`→paid, `failed`/`expired`→failed, `pending`→pending), falling back to the `event_type` mapping when the event has no transaction; `pending` joins the status vocabulary of the list filter. New routes on `paymentsRouter`: `GET /payments/billing-events/:id` (read-only Details — id, member, plan, amount, currency, status, charge type, source, Created/Modified At+By, Next Payment, failure reason, receipt, `can_retry`/`can_record_manual_payment`), `POST /payments/billing-events/:id/retry` and `POST /payments/billing-events/:id/manual-payment` (both `requireModuleWrite('PAYMENTS')`). Neither action ever appends a Billing Event: they add Payment Transactions against the existing `billing_event_id`, so the append-only ledger row is never rewritten. Retry (issue Q3 option B) fires the MIT charge and, on rejection, once more — two rejections pause the assigned plan through `recordStatusChange`, and every covered member's derived payment status follows because `GET /members` now resolves `payment_status` through `user_membership_members` as well as `pr.member_id` (two-person/family plans bill once, against the owner). A successful retry or manual payment advances `next_billing_date`/`last_billed_at` exactly like the nightly run, guarded by a 409 on an event that already has a completed transaction so one charge can never advance the schedule twice. `audit_logs` records the two manual interventions (`retry_payment`, `manual_payment`, added to `AUDIT_ACTIONS`) with previous/new status and the attempt results. The nightly run now also stores the provider's rejection reason on the failed `payment_requests` row, not only in the ledger row's notes. Admin UI: a `ContextMenu` per real row (Details always; Retry Payment + Manual payment only when the row is actionable), Details expanding the same row read-only and the Manual payment card rendered inline inside it — no modal; read-only PAYMENTS roles see the menu with the write items disabled)

- **Done**: #643 (Translate Nutrition Library Content: migration 166 adds `nutrition_library_item_translations` (`item_id FK→nutrition_library_items` CASCADE, `locale`, `name`, PK `(item_id, locale)`, `KEY nlit_locale_name`) — the same catalogue+junction shape as categories (#501) and qualities (#293) — and seeds Spanish + Catalan for all 32 system items (`gym_id IS NULL`) via `INSERT IGNORE ... SELECT` matched on the base name, so a re-run, a renamed item or an already-edited translation is a no-op. `nutrition_library_items.name` stays the base (English) value, the uniqueness key and the fallback for any locale with no row. New `api/src/infra/locale.ts` resolves the request locale from `x-locale`, then `Accept-Language` (q-value ordered, `es-ES`→`es`), then `DEFAULT_LOCALE`, always narrowing to the `SUPPORTED_LOCALES` allowlist (env-configured, default `en,es,ca`); `domain/nutritionLibrary.ts` gains `localizedNameSql`/`localizedNameExpr` (a `COALESCE` subquery, a bare column for the base locale), `loadTranslationsMap`, `replaceTranslations` and `validateTranslations`. Every read surface resolves names per request — `nutrition-library.ts`, `platform-nutrition-library.ts`, `nutrition-plan-templates.ts`, `platform-nutrition-plan-templates.ts`, `member-nutrition-plans.ts` and `me.ts` (`GET /me/nutrition-plan`) — with library list rows now carrying `display_name` (localized) and `translations` next to the base `name`, and list search/ordering following the displayed locale. Superadmins author translations on Cordel → Base Nutrition Library (one field per translatable locale, blank = fall back) through `POST`/`PUT` `/platform/nutrition-library` plus a new `PUT /platform/nutrition-library/:id/translations` sub-resource and `GET /platform/nutrition-library/locales`. Gym-created items keep a single entered name shown in every locale. Category and quality names stay slug-keyed and translated in the apps' locale files, where en/es/ca were already complete. Both apps send `x-locale` from `useLocale()` (added to both proxies' header allowlists). 23 unit cases in `locale.test.ts`; integration coverage added to `platform-nutrition-library.test.ts`, `nutrition-library.test.ts` and `me-nutrition-plan.test.ts`)

- **Done**: #644 (Review and Classify Nutrition Library Nutritional Qualities: migration 167 completes the partial backfill migration 127 (#350) started. `fat` and `fiber` existed in the `nutritional_qualities` catalogue since #501 but had **zero** item assignments, and only 20 `protein`/`carbohydrate` links existed at all. `nutrition_library_items` stores no macro values — the row is `id, gym_id, name, status, image_url, created_at, modified_at` — so, per the ticket thread's answers, the classification is made by food from general nutritional composition and seeded in a migration, where the per-item decisions are reviewable in the diff and versioned with the schema (no numeric columns added; that stays a separate ticket if ever wanted). Stated criterion, indicative per 100 g of the food as normally served: protein ≥ 10 g, carbohydrate ≥ 10 g, fat ≥ 8 g, fiber ≥ 2 g, with wholegrain/whole-plant foods marked for fiber and refined-grain staples not, so the borderline cases are decided by a rule rather than by rounding. All 32 system items are in the table, including the seven that qualify for nothing (Water/Coffee/Tea/Mustard/Hot Sauce/Tomato Sauce/Yogurt Sauce) so the review is complete on its face; each row carries a one-line note. 19 new links: `fat` on Beef/Salmon/Eggs/Tofu/Olive Oil/Nuts/Dairy, `fiber` on Brown Rice/Vegetables/Salad/Sweet Potatoes/Quinoa/Fruit/Oats/Nuts, plus `protein` on Dairy and `carbohydrate` on Juice. Matching is by item name and covers **gym-created** items as well as system ones, but is **additive only** (`INSERT IGNORE`) — re-reviewing 127's 20 links found none that contradict the criterion, and a name-matched heuristic must not delete a tag a gym set by hand; non-active items are skipped, and it is a one-time review rather than a standing rule (an item created later is untouched). Note the `utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci` collation makes the name match case- and accent-insensitive, so a gym's "Salmón" is classified as "Salmon". `down()` is deliberately narrower than `up()` — system items only, and never 127's pairs — because `INSERT IGNORE` keeps no record of which links it actually created, so a name-and-slug delete cannot distinguish its own row from one an admin set by hand; leaving an extra tag on a gym item is recoverable from the UI, deleting a curated one is not. No schema, API, UI or i18n change — the `quality_*` labels and the read/write surface (`domain/nutritionLibrary.ts`, both Nutrition Library pages) already handled all four slugs. New `nutrition-library-quality-classification.test.ts` (6 DB-free unit cases: full system-item coverage, valid slugs only, a note per item, every quality used, no 127 assignment lost, the untagged set), plus 5 integration cases appended to `nutrition-library.test.ts` asserting the seeded result in the migrated database and through `GET /nutrition-library`)

- **Done**: #647 (Book Personal Training Class Slots — all four stages of the plan agreed on the issue thread). **Stage 1 done**: migration 168 adds a nullable `professional_service_id` (FK→`professional_services` `ON DELETE SET NULL`) to both `activity_types` and `calendar_events`, so an occurrence can say which Professional Service (#484) delivers it; the Activity Type is the default and `activity-types.ts` propagates a change to not-yet-started occurrences through the existing `PROPAGATABLE_FIELDS` / `confirm_propagate` flow, `scheduleEngine.materializeScheduleRule()` stamps it on every generated occurrence, and both calendar routers accept it on create/update (a session inherits the Activity Type's value unless the field is sent). New `domain/professionalServices.ts` validates the id against the services visible **and enabled** for the gym (`gym_professional_services.status = 'active'`). New `domain/memberProfessionalServices.ts` answers the Member side — how many sessions the Member holds per Professional Service, summed across purchased `user_class_packages`, `promotion_session` benefits on applied promotions of active assignments, and `user_membership_services` attachments of Session-type items, all resolved through `sellable_item_professional_services` (#546) — served read-only by `GET /members/:memberId/professional-services`. No UI and no booking behaviour change yet. **Stage 2 done**: no migration — `GET /members/:memberId/personal-training-slots` (`api/member-personal-training-slots.ts`) projects the next 2 months of `calendar_events` whose `professional_service_id` the Member holds into recurring Mon–Sun slots, grouped by *gym-local* weekday + start/end time + activity type + professional service (local, not UTC, so a slot survives a DST change). Each slot carries one entry per expected date with a status (`available` / `no_occurrence` / `not_scheduled` / `full` / `already_booked`) plus `available_count` and `fully_available`: per the thread's Q5 answer a taken or unscheduled date is reported, never a reason to drop the weekday from the grid. The grouping rules are pure in `domain/personalTrainingSlots.ts`; #481 eligibility is asked through the new exported `isActivityTypeEligibleForMember()` that the booking hook itself now calls, so the projection can't drift from `bookMemberOnSession`. Admin: display-only `Personal Training Class Slots` section in the expanded Member row (`members/MemberPersonalTrainingSlots.tsx`). **Stage 3 done**: migration 169 adds `member_recurring_slots` — the Member's *weekly pattern* (`iso_weekday` 1=Mon…7=Sun, gym-local `start_time`/`end_time`, activity type, professional service, nullable center), unique through a VIRTUAL `selection_key` that folds a NULL center to 0; `PUT /members/:memberId/personal-training-slots/selections` replaces the whole set (every identity must be a slot the projection currently offers) and `POST /members/:memberId/personal-training-slots/book` books the window for it, optionally replacing the set in the same call. Both are `requireModuleWrite('MEMBERS')`. Booking goes one occurrence at a time through `bookMemberOnSession` in its own transaction, so capacity, waitlist mode, #481 eligibility and package-credit debiting stay the booking path's business and one rejected date cannot roll back the rest; a date that filled up since the projection is rolled back out of the waitlist and reported as `full` (§3: never book a slot that became unavailable), and the report names every date it skipped or failed. A stored slot the grid no longer offers books nothing (§5) and existing bookings are never touched by deselecting. Admin: the same section is now selectable (checkbox per slot, Save selection / Book, per-date result list). **Stage 4 done**: migration 170 adds `recurring_booking_run_log` (a `billing_run_log`-shaped singleton) and extends `chk_member_notifications_type`; `POST /recurring-bookings/run` (`api/recurring-bookings.ts`, `X-Internal-Secret`, fired by `.github/workflows/recurring-booking-run.yml` at 03:00 UTC) maintains the rolling 2-month window. Per the thread's Q4 answer it is an endpoint an external scheduler calls, not an in-process timer — the `POST /billing/run` precedent, and the only shape that behaves when the API runs more than one replica. Each run re-projects every member with stored selections from *now* and books the free dates through the very same `bookSelectedSlots()` the Book button runs (extracted from the stage-3 route, so capacity, waitlist mode, #481 eligibility, credit debiting and the §5 service re-check exist once); the window advances because "now" moved, with no per-member bookkeeping that could drift, and §4's "creates only missing bookings, never duplicates" falls out of the projection. Dates it cannot take raise a `recurring_booking_skipped` member notification (the thread's Q5 answer — festivity, gym closed, or taken by another booking), deduped on (slot, date) over a 120-day lookback so a rolling window does not re-alert nightly; `already_booked` and internal failures never notify. An unscoped run takes the 23-hour lock; a run scoped to a `gym_id`/`member_id` skips it so an operator can re-run one gym after a fix. The job honours the `organization.professional_services` flag, and one member's failure is caught and reported without ending the night's run.

Frontend only: no API, schema or i18n change, and the component's three variants are untouched. The three confirmations left on the default now pass `'success'` — the storage-diagnostics copy in Cordel → Gyms (`system/gyms/page.tsx`) and the manual-payment and successful-retry toasts in Payments → Billing Events. Genuine failures keep the error variant, including `retry_failed` / `retry_failed_paused` and the Nutrition Plan Templates tree's blocking client-side validation messages. - **Done**: #668 (Move Gym Storage Root Under `gyms/`: `buildGymFolderPrefix()` in `api/src/infra/storage.ts` now returns `gyms/<gym_id>-<sanitized_gym_name>` instead of `<gym_id>-<sanitized_gym_name>`, so a gym's root inside the shared R2 bucket is `<bucket>/gyms/<gym_id>-<gym_name>/`. That one helper is the single place the root is built: `POST /platform/gyms/:id/storage/initialize` captures its result in `gyms.storage_folder_prefix`, and both `initializeGymBucket()`'s folder markers and every later `uploadGymImage()` key hang off the stored value — so the `gyms/` root propagates with no second concatenation site and nothing below the gym root changes. Purely an object-key change: bucket (`CLOUDFLARE_R2_BUCKET`), endpoint, credentials, the `{gym_id}-{gym_name}` naming convention, the folder list and the diagnostics/error paths are all untouched, and no migration runs. Per the ticket's explicit constraint, **existing gyms are not migrated** — a gym initialized before this keeps its old captured prefix and its objects (re-running Initialize Cloudflare Bucket deliberately reuses the captured prefix rather than recomputing it), so the bucket can hold both shapes; `docs/go-to-production.md` §1 carries the decision to copy-and-rewrite or accept the split before launch. Unit coverage in `api/src/test/storage.test.ts`: the new prefix shape, every folder marker under `gyms/`, and an upload key under `gyms/`)

Frontend-only deletion: the button, its wrapper `div`, the now-orphaned `handleManage()` helper, and `setActiveGymId` from the page's `useGym()` destructure (it had no other caller in the file). `NOTES` now ends after the notes paragraph with no trailing gap. Nothing else moves — the notes field, the gym information rows, the `Initialize Cloudflare Bucket` button, the Details/Edit/Duplicate/Delete context menu, routing, permissions and the API are untouched, and `setActiveGymId` itself stays in the gym context for the gym switcher and its other callers. - **Done**: #670 (Rename “Amount” to “Price” in Products: the field the Products catalogue calls `amount` is the item's price, so every label that named it now reads **Price** / **Precio** / **Preu** — the `PRICE` list column (still between `UNITS` and `TAX RATE`) and the four form/detail sites that render the field: the inline create form, the inline edit form, the expanded row's `BILLING CONFIGURATION` section and the Details modal. i18n-only: `products.col_amount`/`label_amount` were renamed to `col_price`/`label_price` in en/es/ca — matching the `*_price` key convention the `plans`, `prices`, `class_packages` and `memberships` namespaces already use — and the page reads the new keys. Per the ticket, `Sets` stays an exercise-level field and Target/Unit/Rest on the exercise row are untouched. New `apps/admin/src/test/workout-block-config.test.ts` (20 cases: the per-type map, the minutes and rounds conversions and their round-trip, empty/NaN input → null, hidden columns left to the caller, the three editors routing every write through `blockConfigPatch`, Sets still exercise-level, and the label keys present in all three locales with the retired ones gone))

No confirmation dialog, per the ticket — the delete confirmation is the only one on the page. Base exercises (`gym_id IS NULL`) keep their `Details` + `Clone to gym` menu — the router answers 403 for a gym-side write to them — and the Cordel → Base Exercises page is unchanged. - **Done**: #674 (Payments → Dashboard: a read-only metric page under Payments with four Billing Event counters — Scheduled This Month, and Total / Failed / Successful Last Month. New router `api/src/api/payments-dashboard.ts` exposing a single `GET /payments/dashboard/summary`, mounted in `app.ts` **before** `/payments` so it is gated by its own `payments.dashboard` flag (migration 171 seeds the key enabled) rather than by `payments.transactions` — the Dashboard survives Transactions or Billing Events being switched off, and can be switched off on its own. Last month's three cards come from one grouped query over `billing_events` (`created_at` inside the previous calendar month, one `WHERE be.gym_id = ?`) whose groups are mapped through the shared `deriveBillingEventStatus()` (#640), so the status vocabulary is never re-spelled as SQL and the cards agree with the Billing Events page; `total_last_month` counts every ledger row, so it is not necessarily failed + successful (an `adjustment` or `status_changed` row is neither). Scheduled This Month reuses the page's projection from `user_memberships.next_billing_date` + `billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval`, counting the projected dates that fall inside the current month and skipping dates the nightly run has not caught up with, exactly as the `scheduled` rows on that page do. Month boundaries are derived in UTC in one exported `monthWindows()` so the SQL ranges and the JS projection share a clock, and the API returns the window it counted over so the page's period labels can't drift into the browser's time zone. Frontend `apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/payments/dashboard/page.tsx` follows the #638 Finance Dashboard card wall; the nav entry carries `featureKey: 'payments.dashboard'` and the page itself bounces a direct URL when the flag is off, applying the same superadmin-bypass-unless-impersonating rule as the sidebar. New `payments_dashboard` namespace in en/es/ca; no Billing Event or payment-processing behaviour changed. Covered by `api/src/test/payments-dashboard.test.ts`)

- **Done**: #675 (View Audit Log on every entity's Details view: the deep link #642 added to the Member Details modal is now offered by every Details view in the admin app, through one shared `apps/admin/src/components/ViewAuditLogButton.tsx`. The component owns the permission rule (admin or superadmin, and the `system` / `system.audit` flags, matching the sidebar) and the URL shape (`?entity_type=<canonical>&entity_id=<pk>`), so no Details view hand-rolls either; `scope="platform"` points the three platform-level entities — Gyms, base Themes and the Cordel Nutrition Library — at Cordel → Audit Log instead of the gym one. Filtering is always by id, never by display name. 23 Details views covered: Members, Membership Plans, Promotions, Exercises, Assigned Plans, Tax Rates, Products, Workout Templates, Training Plans and Templates, Activity Types, Professional Services, Centers, Spaces, Staff, Themes, Billing Events, Nutrition Plans and Templates, both Nutrition Libraries, Gyms and platform Themes. On the API side `AUDIT_ENTITY_REGISTRY` gains the nine entity types those views deep-link that `recordAudit()` already wrote but the registry did not know (`activity_type`, `theme`, `tax_rate`, `gym_charge`, `professional_service`, `nutrition_library_item`, `nutrition_plan_template`, `member_nutrition_plan`, `staff`) — without an entry they resolved no `entity_name` and were missing from the `GET /audit-logs/meta` dropdown the deep link preselects. No schema change and no audit-writing behaviour changed. Covered by `apps/admin/src/test/view-audit-log-everywhere.test.ts` and `api/src/test/audit-registry.test.ts`)

- **Done**: #677 (Fix Theme Card Border and Border Radius Settings: the Theme editors' **Cards** group — `cardBorder` (a colour) and `cardBorderRadius` (an `advanced` attribute) — now actually paints the cards, in both Base and Custom Themes. Two defects: `cardBorderRadius` was editable and persisted but never written to any CSS variable, so the Card Border Radius did nothing anywhere; and most card surfaces restated a border colour (`#e2e2e6`, `#ececf0`, `#e0e0e8`) and a `borderRadius` of their own, so even `--gd-card-border` — which `applyTokens()` has always written — only reached the handful of pages that used the variable. `applyTokens()` now emits `--gd-card-radius` through an exported `CARD_ADVANCED_VARS` map, mirroring `CALENDAR_ADVANCED_VARS` and falling back to `DEFAULT_ADVANCED` for a theme with no `advanced` map or a blank value (the calendar's length-fallback logic extracted as the shared `cssLengthValue()`); and every card surface in the admin app — 21 `cardStyle`/`cardSt` definitions plus the inline list cards on Themes, Staff and Centers — spreads one shared `cardSurfaceStyle` (`apps/admin/src/components/ui.tsx`) carrying `border` + `borderRadius` + `background` as `var()`s with their default token values as CSS fallbacks. A highlighted or being-edited card overrides only `border` on top of the spread and keeps the themed radius. Base ↔ Custom is unchanged: both are applied through the same `applyTokens()`, so a Base Theme's values paint when it is the applied theme and a Custom Theme's override when it is (there is still no live sparse inheritance — cloning copies the blob, as since #559). Frontend-only: no migration, no API change, no i18n change, and no other theme setting touched. One deliberate visual change: cards that hardcoded `borderRadius: 10` now follow the token, whose default is `8px`. New `apps/admin/src/test/theme-card-css.test.ts` (7 cases: the attribute → variable map, emission for a configured theme, for a legacy theme with no `advanced` map and for a blank value, the configured-over-default precedence, the Cards colour group's fields, the shared style's variables and its fallback matching the default token, and a source scan asserting no card style declares a border radius or a border colour of its own))

- **Done**: #678 (Unify Base Theme Editor with Custom Theme Editor: expanding a Base Theme row in Cordel → Base Themes (`apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/system/themes/page.tsx`) now opens the same structured editor the Custom Themes screen uses — collapsible **Branding / Colors / Typography** sections over a Save/Cancel footer — instead of a read-only grid of colour swatches that exposed nothing but colours and hid the rest behind a separate *Edit* step. `Assignments` is the one Custom section with no platform counterpart (a theme is assigned to a *gym's* centres). The section wrapper and the Branding block (name, description, logo upload/removal, `logo_contains_gym_name`) moved into a shared `apps/admin/src/components/ThemeSectionEditor.tsx` (`ThemeSection`, `ThemeBrandingEditor`), joining the Colors/Typography editors already shared through `ThemeTokensEditor.tsx`, so a future theme setting is implemented once. On the Custom Themes screen a Base Theme now shows all four sections instead of Assignments + Colours, read-only: read-only means disabled controls, never a different set of controls, so `ThemeAdvancedSection` renders disabled rather than being omitted and `ThemeTypographyEditor` gained the `readOnly` prop `ThemeColorsEditor` already had. No change to the theme engine, persistence, status workflow or Base↔Custom inheritance — same `PUT /platform/themes/:id`, same status select, same #492 draft/live-preview/dirty-guard. Frontend-only: no migration, no API change; `themes.label_description` added to `es.json`/`ca.json` where it was missing and rendered as its raw key. Covered by `apps/admin/src/test/theme-editor-unification.test.ts` (9 cases))

- **Done**: #694 (Website registration no longer fails for a gym with one active and one inactive center: every sole-center fallback now goes through `soleActiveCenterId()` (`api/src/infra/centerContext.ts`) and counts active centers only; for website registration an inactive center behaves exactly like a deleted one (never counts towards "more than one center", explicit inactive `center_id` → same 400 as an unknown id; the self-registration first sign-in falls back to the first active center); the missing-center error now reads `center_id is required as the gym has more than one center`. Staff-side explicit center choices unchanged. No migration.)

- **Done**: #696 (Member app `/link`: setting a password after an invitation no longer fails with "Something went wrong". The password step called `signUp.password({ password, emailAddress })`, and re-sending `emailAddress` un-verified the address the invitation ticket had verified; it now calls `signUp.create({ strategy: 'ticket', ticket, password })`, keeping the ticket-verified email. No API change.)

- **Done**: #698 (Docs: the invitation email is Clerk's single *Invitation* template per instance, with no per-invitation language in Clerk's API — the language is chosen inside the template, see #701. Documented in `docs/wordpress-integration.md`; `docs/go-to-production.md` gains an item to recreate the customised template on the Clerk production instance (a premium feature there). No code change.)

- **Done**: #701 (Invitation language: website registration defaults `locale` to `es`, and copies `ca`/`en` into the invitation's `publicMetadata.lang` so the Clerk *Invitation* template — which picks Catalan/English from `lang` and falls back to Spanish via Handlebars `#if` — and the `/{locale}/link` page always match. `lang` is cleared with `gym_signup` on first sign-in. No migration.)

- **Done**: #702 (Member app `/link`: after a successful `POST /me/link` the page does a full navigation to Home (`window.location.replace`) instead of `router.replace`, so `AppContext` — which loaded `/me/gyms` and `/me/profile` on mount, before the member existed — loads again and the greeting shows the member's name straight after sign-up. No API change.)

- **Done**: #704 (Admin Members list: **Email** column after Name, so website sign-ups — identified by name + email — can be told apart without expanding rows. Frontend only: `GET /members` already returned `email` and search already matched it; new i18n key `members.col_email` en/es/ca.)

- **Done**: #707 (Admin Members list: the Email column no longer wraps one character per line — `word-break: break-all` let `DataTable`'s auto layout squeeze it to ~85px even at 1440px. Plain text like System → Users, with a `<wbr>` after `@` so narrow screens split it in two at most.)

- **Done**: #709 (Orphaned Clerk accounts: one shared definition of a *linked* login — superadmin, staff `gym_memberships`, or an active member — in `infra/clerk-account-links.ts`. (1) `DELETE /members/:id` deletes the Clerk user first when it was its last link and always removes this gym's member-role `gym_memberships` row; (2) the Clerk `user.deleted` webhook unlinks every Gymdesk row; (3) superadmin *Cordel → Orphaned accounts* lists unlinked logins with a reason and deletes them (re-checked, Clerk first, audited). Members page Delete/Revoke moved from `confirm()` to `ConfirmDialog`. No migration.)

- **Done**: #636 (Payment Providers moved from Finance to Cordel and became real data: migration 175 adds a platform-level `payment_providers` catalogue (no `gym_id` — like `themes`/`charge_types`) with `provider_key` (the adapter `getPaymentProvider()` switches on), a soft-delete, a **single default** enforced by a generated-column unique index, and `gyms.payment_provider_id` **NOT NULL** with a RESTRICT foreign key, backfilled to the seeded `MONEI` row. Superadmin CRUD at `/platform/payment-providers` (inline card UX, no modal) plus `GET .../deployment`, which reports the env-derived credential status the retired Finance page used to render — **credentials stay in environment variables**, the catalogue stores none. Deleting a provider gyms point at is a 409, as is deactivating one; the default cannot be deleted or un-flagged (another provider takes the flag). Cordel → Gyms gains a mandatory **Payment Provider** dropdown, pre-populated with the default. The Finance nav entry, the `/payments/payment-providers` redirect and the `financials.payment_providers` feature flag are gone. Billing itself still resolves the adapter from `PAYMENT_PROVIDER`; pointing it at the gym's row is deferred until a second adapter exists — see `docs/go-to-production.md`.)

- **Done**: #726 (Audit Log labels: en/es/ca translations for the `system` and `provider` sources and for the 25 audit actions the API writes without a label (e.g. `restore`, `activate`, `refund_credit`, `impersonation_started`); Source filter gains System/Provider; `AUDIT_ACTIONS` gains the 21 written-but-unlisted actions so they can be filtered.)

- **Done**: #729 (CI: the #709 audit-row tests poll with a new `eventually()` test helper instead of a fixed 50 ms sleep (fire-and-forget audit writes were slower on CI); Deploy API prunes dangling podman images before its free-disk check — deploys had been aborting on a full VPS disk since #721.)

- **Done**: #733 (Docs: `architecture.md` release steps mention the API deploy's dangling-image prune and 1 GB free check (#729); the CI/CD secrets table lists `CLERK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` — must equal the Clerk endpoint's signing secret, endpoint enabled and subscribed to `user.created` + `user.deleted`.)

- **Done**: #676 (Activities list: the colour bullet leaves the name cell and becomes a **Colour** column of its own — swatch + hex — between a new truncated **Description** column and *Created by*, the column order the Plans page uses. An Activity with no colour shows `—`, never a fallback swatch; the editable Colour field in the inline edit form and the add row is untouched. Frontend only; new i18n keys `activity_types.col_description` / `col_color` en/es/ca.)

- **Done**: #634 (Member Membership Configuration and Consolidated Billing Simulation — stage 3 of the #629 → #631 → #634 chain, whose engine and Additional Periodic Services shipped in the first two. Migration 172 narrows migration 007's one-active-Membership-per-Member unique index from `(active_member_key)` to `(active_member_key, membership_plan_id)`, so a Member may hold **several active Membership Plans in parallel, one per Plan** — the thread's answer to the blocking question. The index swap needs no data migration (the old guarantee implies the new one) and touches no row; the rollback refuses with an actionable message if any Member already holds two. Adding a Plan is purely additive (§14): `POST /user-memberships` no longer supersedes anything, and "Assign New Plan" stays as the separate, explicit supersede action. `POST /user-memberships` and `/:id/assign-new-plan` now reject a Plan that is not Active + Public (§2, `planAssignabilityError()`); `GET /membership-plans` gains an `enrollment_status` filter, and the Members page's plan fetch — previously `?status=active`, a parameter the endpoint does not read, so every plan came back — now asks for `lifecycle_status=active&enrollment_status=public`. New read-only `GET /user-memberships/member/:memberId/configuration` (`member-membership-configuration.ts`) returns `{plans, promotions, services}` in one call — every assignment newest-first with `is_live` and its `activity_allowances`, plus the live assignments' Promotions and Additional Services, each carrying `user_membership_id` and `plan_name`. It contains no business logic: every write stays on the existing per-Assigned-Plan routes. `MemberExpandedRow.tsx` is restructured into the ticket's four independent sections — `MEMBERSHIP PLANS` (`MemberMembershipPlans.tsx`, inline "+ Add Membership Plan" with radio-button Plan selection), `PROMOTIONS` (`MemberPromotions.tsx`, Member-level, each row naming its Plan, stacking rules live in the picker), `ADDITIONAL SERVICES` (`MemberAdditionalServices.tsx`, reusing #631's inline editor once per live Plan) and `BILLING SIMULATION` — none nested inside a plan card (§13) and no modal anywhere (§15). A change in any of the first three re-reads the configuration and remounts the simulation (§12), whose lines now name the Plan that generated each charge (§7). The simulation itself already consolidated every live assignment from #629/#631, so §5–§11 needed no engine change. Knock-on: `POST /me/payment-requests` stopped picking "the" active membership with `LIMIT 1` — it takes an optional `user_membership_id` and answers `409 multiple_active_memberships` otherwise. New `members.*` keys in en/es/ca; new `api/src/test/member-membership-configuration.test.ts`, plus cases in `user-memberships.test.ts`, `membership-plans.test.ts` and `membership-promotions` coverage for parallel plans, the Active+Public gate and the new-members rule. §3's "Promotion `Only applicable for new members` is respected" landed as a follow-up once the thread defined "new": *a member that books his/her first membership plan in 12 months* — a Member away for longer than that counts as new again. `domain/newMemberEligibility.ts` holds the rule (an Assigned Plan disqualifies its Member when it is still live, started inside the window, or ended/was closed inside it; a row superseded by Assign New Plan carries no end date and is dated by the assignment that replaced it) and `api/new-member-eligibility.ts` reads the Member's assignments for it. Enforced at every apply path — `POST /user-memberships/:id/promotions`, `applyPromotionToMembership()` (assign-new-plan, `POST /membership-plans/:id/assign`'s auto-apply, `POST /payments/apply-promotion`) and #628's up-front `validatePromotionSelection()`, which now takes the `member_id` — always excluding the assignment the Promotion is being applied to, so a Member's first Plan never disqualifies itself. The configuration read reports it per plan as `new_member_eligible`, which is what the PROMOTIONS picker disables the option on. No migration: the column already exists. Note the flag is `DEFAULT 1`, so every Promotion created before this carries it — test fixtures that are not about the rule now set it to 0 explicitly. Also unchanged, and out of scope: the Assigned Plan snapshot architecture (#635).)

- **Done**: #712 (Custom Themes header shows who created a Theme, when, and whether it is the gym's Theme: migration 178 adds the `created_by_name`/`created_by_type` actor snapshot to `themes` (the same immutable-snapshot shape as `themes.deleted_by_name` and `tax_rates.created_by_name`), backfilled from the earliest `create`/`clone` `audit_logs` entry per theme; `POST /system/themes/clone/:sourceId` — the only path that creates a Customer Theme — captures it, and every `/system/themes` response gains `created_by_name` plus `is_gym_theme`, derived from `gyms.theme_id` for the *requesting* gym. The Customer Themes card header renders Created by · Created at for Custom Themes and a ★ Gym theme badge on the assigned one; Base Theme rows and the editor sections are unchanged.), #713 (Custom Theme logos move out of the `themes.logo_bytes` MEDIUMBLOB into the gym's Cloudflare R2 folder: `POST /system/themes/:id/logo` uploads to `<gyms.storage_folder_prefix>/Branding/Logo/logo.<ext>` — extension from the validated MIME type, never from the file name — and migration 180 adds `themes.logo_object_key` plus `CHECK chk_themes_logo_storage`; the public URL is derived at read time and exposed as `logo_url` on every theme-shaped response (`/system/themes`, `/gyms`, `/me/gym`, `/me/gyms`), which both app headers and the Custom Themes page now prefer, with `GET /themes/:id/logo` still serving either storage mode. The key names the gym, so an upload hands the branding slot over to the uploading theme and deletes the object it replaces. Base Theme logos and Custom logos uploaded before #713 stay blob-backed until replaced; `logo_contains_gym_name` still decides whether the gym name renders next to the logo.), #725 (Custom Themes configure the six **Members App background images** — My Training Plan, My Nutrition, Calendar, My Bookings, My Membership and the general background: migration 181 adds `theme_member_images` (one row per configured `(theme_id, slot)`, carrying the R2 object key and `gym_id`), `POST`/`DELETE /system/themes/:id/members-images/:slot` upload and clear a slot, and every theme-shaped response gains `members_images` — six fields, `null` for an unconfigured slot, resolved to URLs by the new `domain/themeMemberImages.ts` with the `?v=` stamp the logo already uses. The object key is derived server-side from the tenant's own `storage_folder_prefix`, the theme row and the slot — `<prefix>/Themes/<theme_id>-<name>/Members/<slot>.png` — so the uploaded file name never reaches it and a gym cannot aim an upload anywhere but its own folder; the missing folder markers are written first, idempotently. Uploads are PNG/JPEG/WebP ≤ 4 MB, validated by signature rather than by the `Content-Type` header, and a refused upload leaves the existing image and its reference untouched. **Remove clears the row only** — #725 requires the object to survive it — so a re-upload reuses the same deterministic key. In the Theme editor the new *Members App* section stages picks and removals in the draft and performs them on Save, the lifecycle the logo has had since #188; in the member app `components/MembersBackground.tsx` paints the slot the current route maps to onto `<body>` as centred `cover` artwork under a scrim of the theme's own `pageBackground`, and a `null` slot leaves the theme background colour. Base Themes are out of scope (six nulls) and a clone starts unconfigured, since cloning has never copied a theme's R2 assets.), #728 (the six Members App theme images become the **backgrounds of the Members home sections**: the Calendar / My Training Plan / My Bookings / My Nutrition tiles and the My Membership card each paint their own slot, over the general `background` slot #725 already paints on the page. Frontend-only — no migration, no API change, no request per section: the URLs are already on the `theme.members_images` payload the app holds. New `apps/member/src/components/MembersSectionCard.tsx` renders the element the page already rendered (a tile `<button>`, the membership `<div role="button">`) with the caller's own styles and replaces only its `background`, so the grid, the card dimensions, the icons, the labels and the navigation are untouched; a card's artwork is `scroll` rather than the page's `fixed` (a viewport-anchored image would show a different crop in every card) under a scrim of the theme's own `cardBackground` at 82 %, heavier than the page's 72 % because the text sits directly on it. A `null` slot leaves the card exactly as it is today — the member app still resolves no fallback of its own. The Next Booking and Alerts cards deliberately keep their plain surfaces. 17 structural/unit cases in the member app's `members-section-backgrounds.test.ts`.), #732 (**Base Themes configure the same six Members App background images**: migration 182 widens `theme_member_images.gym_id` to nullable so a row can belong to the platform, and a Base Theme's images live in the platform's own folder — `cordel/Themes/<theme_id>-<sanitized theme name>/Members/<slot>.png`, built from the new `PLATFORM_STORAGE_ROOT` (`cordel`, the sibling of #668's `gyms/` root) by the *same* `buildThemeMemberImageKey()`. `POST`/`DELETE /platform/themes/:id/members-images/:slot` (superadmin) mirror the gym routes of #725 — signature-validated PNG/JPEG/WebP ≤ 4 MB, the four folder markers written first, the deterministic key derived server-side, Remove clearing the row and leaving the object — and resolve the theme with `gym_id IS NULL`, so each router 404s on the other's themes. Every `/platform/themes` payload carries `members_images`; on the gym side `shapeTheme()` stops forcing a Base Theme to six nulls and `loadMemberImagesByTheme()` includes `gym_id IS NULL` rows for theme ids the caller already named, so `GET /system/themes` shows them read-only and `/me/gym(s)` — and therefore `MembersBackground`/`MembersSectionCard` — paint them with no member-app change at all. The Base Theme editor (Cordel → Themes) gains the same **Members App** section, reusing #725's `ThemeMembersImagesEditor` with its stage-until-Save draft. A clone still starts unconfigured. 25 integration cases in `base-theme-members-images.test.ts`, 4 unit cases for the platform keys, 8 structural cases in the admin app's `base-theme-members-images.test.ts`.), #735 (**Gym Bucket Initialization creates the gym-level `Themes/` folder**: `POST /platform/gyms/:id/storage/initialize` now writes one more zero-byte marker — `gyms/<gym_id>-<sanitized_gym_name>/Themes/` — appended to `GYM_FOLDERS` so every folder that existed before keeps the position it was written in. No migration and no new endpoint: the action already rewrites the whole marker set under the prefix captured on first init, so a gym provisioned before this ticket receives the folder by re-running it, and re-running stays idempotent (every key ends in `/`, so the object a marker overwrites is always another marker, never a file). The root only — a Custom Theme's `Themes/<theme_id>-<name>/Members/` branch cannot exist before the theme does and stays with `ensureStorageFolders()` at upload time (#725). `'Themes'` now has one spelling, `THEMES_FOLDER` in `infra/storage.ts`, which `domain/themeMemberImages.ts` re-exports as its `THEME_STORAGE_FOLDER`. 4 unit cases added to `storage.test.ts` (the marker is written, it is the same folder a theme folder prefix is built from, nothing theme-specific is created below it, a second run writes the same keys as empty markers) and 1 integration case in `gyms-storage.test.ts` for re-initializing a gym provisioned before the folder existed.)

- **Done**: #718 (Import Base Exercises into Gym Exercises: the gym Exercises page's **Import Defaults** button — which seeded eight hardcoded names — becomes **Import**, opening an *Import Exercises* modal over the platform Base Exercises library. Two new gym-facing endpoints: `GET /exercises/base?q=&muscle=` lists active library rows with their muscles and an `imported_exercise_id` marking the gym's own copy (matched on `cloned_from_id` *or* name, so pre-#718 copies aren't offered again), and `POST /exercises/import` copies `{ baseExerciseIds }` in one transaction, keeping each base name (no "(Copy)" suffix) and recording `cloned_from_id`. Ids that aren't active base exercises — including another gym's custom exercise — are a 400 with `invalid_ids`; ids the gym already has come back as `skipped`. The modal filters server-side, keeps its selection across filter changes, restricts *Select all matching* to the filtered importable rows, and shows already-imported rows disabled.
